# Polling and timed-wait inventory

Baseline: `d33ed17571`. Locations refer to that baseline so deletion does not erase the audit trail.

Scope: `rust/crates/**` and `typescript/packages/**`, excluding the separately owned `harness`, `harness-core`, `harness-tui` and `harness-client` packages. Product code, tests, conformance, examples, benchmarks and scripts are included.

Candidates include event-driven loops and retry identity references; these require classification, not mechanical removal. Pending rows are not approval to retain a timer. The only timing exceptions are caller-chosen request deadlines and backoff following transient transport failure. Tool/model retry and exact idempotent resend identities are allowed. Actor alarm implementations are the event primitive itself.

## Logical capacity audit

Audit basis: design audit v3 (2026-10-30), axis (f), and gap register G63â€“G67. This inventory distinguishes limits on complete logical work from pages, scheduling credit, physical protocol constraints and explicit caller capacity. Status starts as **open**; completion requires removal of the refusal/truncation and its hidden default, with consumer updates.

Excluded: `harness`, `harness-core`, `harness-tui`, `harness-client`, and `typescript/packages/harness` (tracked separately in #1744). MCP startup/tool defaults, agent depth/goals/checkpoints/children/history and the shell preset in those packages remain in that scope. The filesystem shell adapter must preserve the optional caller deadline supplied by that work.

Explicit small-capacity atomicity/replay/funding controls retained. Fixed-key namespace/replay indexing obligations remain pending. |

Original append and exact replay survive reopen beyond a selected compaction target. Allowed bounds retained after inspection:

- Directory, result, event and output range page sizes split work and expose continuation.
- Active runtime slots, in-flight requests and byte credit delay admission without discarding logical intent.
- Cache capacity evicts reproducible data without limiting logical history.
- OS-enforced component/argv constraints apply only in the corresponding native adapter.
- Explicit caller reservations, capacities and deadlines remain explicit; absence must remain unbounded.
- Protective framing/decompression bounds need a concrete physical encoding or bomb-protection contract; a small logical envelope is not protection.

Validation obligations for each changed mechanism: preserve atomicity and authority; waits name their actual producer and report producer loss; admission/check overhead is constant in unrelated history/population. Negative controls restore a former cap, suppress/drop the producer, or grow unrelated history. Full requested output necessarily costs its own bytes/items; this is not a population scan. Record required syscalls, durability barriers, round trips, allocations and copies in the PR, including before/after counts where known.

## Native batch arrival

`NativeWatch::wait_for_changes_with_native_work` observes the original retained queue and native-batch boundary on the watch's existing condition variable. It does not consume hints or write a fence cookie. Inotify brackets the same serialized drain, so an arrival observer cannot seal a half-delivered rename. Its original delivery sink also receives permanent producer loss; both physical retirement paths publish that terminal before withdrawing their original owner. A rescan can clear ordinary continuity loss, but cannot resurrect a physically retired producer. Native source callback reentry and partial startup return their retained typed failure.

Safety controls use real file writes and a rename, with a physically settled early batch as the event-before-subscribe negative control. Cancellation and physical inotify retirement race the original arrival registration without a timer. Progress has one producer: completion of that original native batch, or its retained loss/cancellation terminal. The Linux named control also asserts that waiting adds zero backend reads and writes. The native library compiled on Linux x86_64 with `cargo check --locked -p acyclic-fs --lib` (39.30 s compiler time); execution of this new control remains pending.

Required per-operation work: the existing queue and condition remain one each, with no added worker, task, queue, scan, durability barrier, round trip or payload copy. A cancellable blocked observer retains the existing O(1) cancellation registration and notification allocation. The two inotify batch-boundary locks are fixed work per native drain, independent of queued history; no idle native event means no condition notification. Remaining consumer polling rows stay pending until they use this event.

## Rejecting source rule

Pending: `scripts/check-no-polling.mjs <baseline-revision>` compares real Git objects with tracked and untracked sources in the working tree. `scripts/no-polling.mjs` rejects new timer/readiness references, aliases, embedded timer programs and unclassified loops. JavaScript/TypeScript uses an explicitly declared compiler-API parser dependency; native sources retain raw-string and nested-comment boundaries. Each disposition names one exact source fingerprint, classification, producer, terminal and negative control. Changing executable tokens reopens dispositions in that source unit; an accepted occurrence cannot authorize another copy.

The source controls cover direct timers, imported and assigned aliases, wrapped timer values, computed property concatenation, dynamic calls, template substitutions, regex boundaries, escaped identifiers, rechecks, child-status probes and timed condition waits. JavaScript/TypeScript dependency controls additionally cover renamed/namespace/default imports, re-exports, escaped object methods, lexical shadowing, recursive helper groups, changed helpers with unchanged callers, module side effects and lost local source. Dependencies identify declarations, not shared function spellings. An exact disposition for a helper does not authorize a new caller. Unresolved relative/workspace imports remain candidates for review rather than being silently exempted.

Both snapshots reuse one parsed AST per script source. Native token streams are released after recording their sites. The dependency graph is finite source analysis; it has no timer, periodic task, generated code or per-reference I/O. Graph traversal visits each edge a fixed number of times; canonical fingerprint ordering additionally sorts component members and outgoing digests. No constant-time claim is made for the complete source analysis. Git subprocesses increase from three to four: one baseline tree enumeration preserves deleted producers, with all original blobs still read through one batch. Controls use actual Git objects, including staged producer deletion and untracked consumers.

All 13 source controls passed on Linux x86_64 with `node --test scripts/no-polling.test.mjs` (2.10 s), after declaring the parser dependency and generating the lockfile with the actual package manager. Required-check integration, exact disposition review, native cross-file bindings, dynamic initialization and external export resolution remain pending; this source checkpoint does not close the static-rule obligation. Run the contributor command after installing dependencies: `bun run check:no-polling origin/main`.

The local durable-root reopening control also consumes `OwnershipReleaseBarrier::released()` directly before reacquiring the original provider locks. This replaces the later-added ten-second deadline and blocking-wait task at `filesystem/src/tests/facade/local.rs`; no timer or worker is needed to observe final provider destruction.

The crash-left fence contention control also deletes its later-added five-second output wait and 16 KiB retained-output ceiling. Original stdout is consumed once into a sink after readiness; physical exit remains owned by `ProcessTree`. Readiness and release use two pipes per child, with no temporary files, status probes, extra reader thread, payload retention or product resource. The lost-release negative control adds one actual child invocation; the original two contenders remain.
